# Bramble Studio — 1-1 checklist and self-audit

The goal is Logic Pro 13, 1-1 in every way: the same windows, menus, right-click menus, key
commands, drags and gestures, animations, editors, instruments, effects and Session Players. It
also opens and saves real `.logicx` and GarageBand `.band` projects. Built from scratch on the
suite's shared code (kn-ui: window frame, pointer fixes, themes, liquid glass, Sfera fonts).

Status: `[x]` done and checked, `[~]` partly there (the note says what's missing), `[ ]` not started.
The item-by-item lists at the bottom (menus, right-click menus, key commands) are generated by
`tool/checklist.py`; re-run it after each change. The audit below is written by hand.

## Honest audit (2026-11-10)

The first draft opened and played real projects, but most of Logic was missing: about 6% of
the menu bar, 2% of the key commands, no right-click menus, one effect, and a General MIDI
SoundFont in place of every instrument. The drummer was a stub. Everything below is the way to 1-1.

## 1. Look and suite parity
- [~] Dark appearance, Logic's colours, Sfera fonts. Measured on screenshots; light mode untested
- [x] Liquid glass and the suite's capsules (kn_ui::capsule: glass / classic / dark rimmed) on the control bar
- [x] Appearance, colour theme, liquid glass and title bar in Settings ▸ View (per-app look file)
- [x] Window frame, title bar, resize edges, HiDPI pointer, Wayland pinch: from kn-ui's winframe and the patched winit
- [~] Help tags on region, cycle, note, pan, XY and master drags; Show/Hide Help Tags. Quick Help (⇧/, Help menu, control-bar ?): the area under the pointer described (control bar, toolbar, Browser, Inspector, ruler, headers, tracks, editor, Mixer, right panes; Studio's own words) in the Inspector, a floating window or a bubble (Quick Help appears as). [ ] help tags for the rest of Logic's drags, Quick Help for single controls (it describes areas, not each button)
- [~] Animations: the research measured Logic's pane show/hide (Browser, Inspector, Editor) and key zoom as instant (one frame, UI-ANIMATIONS.txt, GESTURES-SCROLL.txt), and Studio does the same. [ ] region drop, playhead catch scrolling and Live Loops queue rings not measured in Logic yet
- [x] App icon: the gold record (data/icons, installed with the menu shortcut); the About window (record, version, credits, website)
- [~] Icons: every icon Studio drew by hand is now a glyph of the Bramble Icons font (Phosphor + Tabler at one line weight, plus Studio's own tuning fork, drum, MIDI port, Flex and Fade tools in tool/icons): control bar, transport, modes, right panes, track headers (keys, drums, guitar / bass, synth, strings, microphone or waveform, picked from the track's name and patch), tool menus, local bars, Settings tabs, New Track cards, Mixer / plug-in bypass, window buttons; filled symbols for lit buttons. Checked headless, dark and light. [ ] Logic's own pictures (Assign Track Icon…, its icon set) aren't there; no bass-guitar or violin glyph (guitar and notes stand in)

## 2. Main window
- [~] Control bar as Logic 13's: view buttons, transport (Stop / Go to Beginning with its Stop Button Options, Play, Record, Cycle; Capture as Recording, Replace and Autopunch when ticked in Customize Control Bar and Display…), LCD with Beats & Project / Beats & Time / Time and its menus, Low Latency, Tuner (the Tuner window: the input's note, cents on a ±50 arc, Hz, reference pitch; pitch found by a YIN-style detector, tested), Solo, Count-in, Metronome (2026-11-12, studio-polish: the button's icon drawn so its arm swings while playing with the click on, one pendulum swing per beat ending on each beat, following tempo changes, upright when stopped; repaints only while swinging; angle maths tested), master volume, right-pane buttons. [ ] the Tuner reads the audio input (LG_TEST_INPUT for checks), not a track's output as Logic's does; Logic's Tuner look not in the research (Studio's own)
- [~] Toolbar (⌃⌥⌘T, View ▸ Show Toolbar, the control bar's button): a row of command buttons under the control bar; Customize Toolbar… (and its right-click) picks them, kept in ~/.config/bramble-studio/toolbar.txt. Customize Control Bar and Display… (View menu, the LCD's ⌄ menu): View panes, Capture as Recording / Replace / Autopunch (controlbar::Shown), the LCD's display; Apply / Save As Control Bar and Display Defaults. [ ] Logic's exact toolbar button set and icons (not in the research: Studio's labelled buttons; Section buttons are disabled until those commands exist), the sheet's other Logic options (Rewind / Forward buttons, Low Latency, Solo, Count-in, Master Level visibility), DisplayState's transportLayoutDict / actionBarLayoutDict IDs not decoded
- [~] Navigation (nav.rs, tested): Go to Position… / Marker Number… / Set Left and Right Locator numerically… sheets (bar beat division tick or minutes:seconds), rewind / forward by division, 1/8, 1/16, nudge value and frame, Play from Beginning / Previous Bar / Left or Right Locator / Left Window Edge, last locate position, Play Button Options and Auto Set Locators (Navigate menu), locators by playhead (rounded), by selection, and Play / Cycle Play, move by 1 bar, Go to Marker Number 1–20, Create Marker by Cycle Area, Create Arrangement Marker, division and nudge values, Tap Tempo (one undo step); zoom: ≈1.3× a key press (measured), Zoom to fit Selection (h, v, both), All Contents, Locators, Toggle Zoom to fit (again: back), Save as / Recall Zoom 1–3, navigation snapshots with Back / Forward (⌥Z, ⌥⇧Z). [ ] Shuttle and Scrub, Rewind / Forward by Transient, Toggle Cycle Preview, Horizontal / Vertical Auto Zoom, Cycle Through Window Views (Tab: keys follow the pointer here)
- [~] Screensets (screensets.rs, tested): 1–9 and ⇧ + two digits for 11–99, made from the window as it is when a number is new; unlocked ones keep changes, locked come back as locked; the Window menu lists them (✓ the current one) with Duplicate…, Rename…, Delete, Lock / Unlock, Revert to Saved; Next / Previous Screenset. A project's own screensets are read from its DisplayState.plist (lg-core displaystate, tested on the research's samples: numbers, names, lock, Browser / Inspector / List Editors / control bar / toolbar / bottom editor and its height). [ ] screensets aren't written back into the .logicx (Studio keeps them for the session); floating windows aren't part of a screenset; the bare digit keys go to screensets (Logic's key list binds them to markers 1–9 too: Go to Marker Number n works from ⌥/ and other bindings)

## 3. Editing
- [~] Key focus (focus.rs, tested; checked headless): the area clicked last (Tracks area, the editor, Mixer, Browser, Event List, Step Editor, windows, Smart Controls, Project Audio) gets Delete, Select All, Cut / Copy / Paste, the arrow keys and every key command, wherever the pointer is. Menus and popovers keep it; ⇥ / ⇧⇥ (Cycle Through Window Views) move it; Logic's 2 pt blue frame marks it. Delete in an editor removes its selected notes or steps, never the region (the reported "deleting a note deletes the track" bug: the pointer used to pick the area). ⌘C / ⌘X / ⌘V from the keyboard reach the commands (egui-winit sends them as clipboard events, which Studio used to ignore). Region Copy / Paste takes every selected region on every track, pasted at the playhead with their track offsets and spacing (regionclip.rs). ⌃ key bindings no longer fire on the bare key. [ ] ⌘V does nothing when the system clipboard is empty (egui-winit sends no event); the focus frame isn't checked in the light theme
- [~] Piano Roll tools (rolltools.rs, pianoroll.rs, tested): notes land in the row clicked (the pitch under the pointer was one row too high: row_at had +1); the pointer takes the tool's shape over the grid with Logic's hotspot (Pencil / Eraser / Brush tip, Finger fingertip, centred tools; Marquee crosshair, Zoom magnifier); the Brush paints along the drag at the Time Quantize steps following the pointer's pitch (Shift: the pitch it started on), also starting on a note; Pencil Shift-drag paints, Control-Shift-click ignores Snap; Pointer drag on the background is a rubber band (Shift adds); the Eraser deletes what it's dragged over; Mute toggles (the clicked note's state for all selected), Solo plays the note, Zoom click / Option-click, Velocity Option-Shift sets all to one, the Finger tool (F) resizes from anywhere on a note; double-click makes the default note.
